## `AFG-9499-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Donation Audit Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Philanthropic Fund Blockchain Immutable Traceability Audit

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Satellite Telecom Operator
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Environmental NGO Conservation Patrol Vessel Dynamic Positioning
- **Intro:** Satellite Telecom Operator — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Environmental NGO Conservation Patrol Vessel Dynamic Positioning — Cold Check Initiation and System Isolation: Main power off: 0 VAC at all DP breakers. LOTO tags applied to all isolation points. Vessel position accuracy: ±10 m for reference.
